### [1] [TileFuse: A Fused Mixed-Precision Kernel Library for Efficient Quantized LLM Inference on AMD NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11357)
*Wesley Pang,Gregory Hyegang Jun,Feiyang Liu,Deming Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TileFuse是一个面向AMD XDNA2 NPU的近金属级混合精度内核库，成功将AWQ等实用低比特量化格式直接部署到边缘设备上，在LLM推理中实现了相比全精度基线高达121.6%（GEMM）和281%（GEMV）的性能提升，以及端到端场景下2.0倍的延迟降低和64.6%的能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘设备上LLM推理需求的增长，NPU被广泛集成以提升性能和能效。然而，当前客户端NPU的实际LLM部署面临重大挑战：AWQ等主流量化格式无法良好映射到现有的NPU软件栈上，这些软件栈通常具有专有性质且低级控制能力有限，迫使模型必须根据NPU特定的量化方案进行重构。

Method: TileFuse协同设计权重布局、元数据放置、混合精度微内核和阵列级数据流。具体技术包括：(1)将解包、反量化和GEMM/GEMV执行融合为单一内核流程；(2)引入支持高达32K GEMM维度的交错预分块布局；(3)重新设计GEMV数据流以充分利用4x8 AIE阵列。该方法使AWQ风格的W4A16和W8A16格式能够直接在XDNA2上运行。

Result: 在内核级评估中，TileFuse相比全精度基线实现了GEMM最高121.6%、GEMV最高281%的性能提升，且在GEMM上相比强iGPU基线获得了超过2倍的性能和能效增益。在Ryzen AI笔记本的端到端LLM实验中，TileFuse实现了高达2.0倍的预填充延迟降低，同时能耗降低超过64.6%。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明XDNA2是AWQ风格边缘LLM推理的可行目标平台，通过为现成量化方案提供原生NPU支持，可以显著提升NPU在实际客户端部署中的可用性，证明了在NPU上直接支持主流量化格式的实用价值。

Abstract: With the growing demand for on-device LLM inference, edge SoCs increasingly integrate NPUs to improve performance and energy efficiency under tight power and thermal budgets. However, practical LLM deployment on current client NPUs remains difficult: widely used quantization formats such as AWQ do not map cleanly onto many existing NPU software stacks, which are often proprietary and expose limited low-level control. In this work, we present \textit{TileFuse}, a close-to-metal mixed-precision kernel library for AMD XDNA2 NPUs that targets transformer linear layers in quantized LLM inference. TileFuse brings practical low-bit formats such as AWQ-style W4A16 and W8A16 directly onto XDNA2, rather than forcing the model to be reshaped around an NPU-specific quantization scheme.
  TileFuse co-designs weight layout, metadata placement, mixed-precision microkernels, and array-level dataflow. Specifically, it fuses unpacking, dequantization, and GEMM/GEMV execution into a single kernel flow, introduces an interleaved pre-tiling layout that supports GEMM dimensions up to 32K, and redesigns GEMV dataflow to utilize the full 4x8 AIE array. Across kernel-level evaluations, TileFuse improves performance by up to 121.6% for GEMM and 281% for GEMV over full-precision baselines, while delivering more than 2x performance and energy-efficiency gains over strong iGPU baselines on GEMM. In end-to-end LLM experiments on Ryzen AI laptops, TileFuse achieves up to 2.0x lower prefilling latency with more than 64.6% lower energy consumption. Together, these results show that XDNA2 is a practical target for AWQ-style edge LLM inference and that native NPU support for off-the-shelf quantization can make NPUs substantially more usable in real client deployments.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond Per-Token Pricing: A Concurrency-Aware Methodology for LLM Infrastructure Cost Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11690)
*Chitral Patil*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 现有公共LLM成本计算器假设GPU利用率为固定值，这是主要误差来源；本文提出测量方法C_eff = f(H, M, Q, lambda, L)，并发布开源工具vllm-cost-meter，可实时测量真实成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共LLM成本计算器将GPU利用率视为用户输入或预设值（通常假设100%），而非基于实际负载测量。这种假设导致成本估算严重失真，特别是在低流量工作负载下，系统性地低估自托管成本并误导决策。

Method: 提出参数化测量方法论C_eff = f(H, M, Q, lambda, L)，其中lambda是运营商可控的请求率；在H100和A100 80GB PCIe硬件上，对稠密模型、超稀疏MoE和稀疏MoE模型进行42+56次基准测试验证；发布开源工具vllm-cost-meter，可附加到运行中的vLLM服务器并报告真实$/M-tokens成本。

Result: 在相同H100硬件上，实际成本跨度为$0.21-$15.25/M-tokens；低到中等企业负载(1-10 rps)下利用不足惩罚为2.5-24x，接近空闲时高达36.3x；FP8量化对MoE架构的收益比稠密模型高2.2-2.4x（+69~+74% vs +31%峰值吞吐量）；活跃参数数量（非总模型大小）是饱和经济学的主要预测因子；在A100 PCIe上验证，负载驱动差异为7.0-11.4x，结果可复现。

Conclusion: 任何忽略利用率的成本估算都会系统性低估真实成本达1/U倍；活跃参数数量是成本预测的关键指标；FP8量化收益因架构而异，且在无原生FP8张量核心的硬件上可能反转；框架已考虑硬件条件限制。

Abstract: Every public LLM cost calculator we surveyed treats GPU utilization as a fixed input -- entered by the user, baked in as a preset, or silently assumed at 100% -- never measured against the operator's actual load. We show that this assumption is the dominant source of error: on identical H100 hardware, effective cost spans \$0.21 to \$15.25 per million output tokens, an underutilization penalty of 2.5-24x across low-to-moderate enterprise loads (1-10 rps) and up to 36.3x near idle -- driven by one operator-controlled variable, offered request rate lambda, which sets in-flight concurrency via Little's Law and which no open-source calculator exposes. Because calculators take utilization as a user-supplied input, any utilization-naive estimate understates true cost by exactly 1/U, systematically mispricing self-hosting -- most severely over-selling it for low-traffic workloads. We propose a measurement methodology that parameterizes the relationship as C_eff = f(H, M, Q, lambda, L), validate it with 42 benchmarks across dense, ultra-sparse MoE, and sparse MoE models, and release vllm-cost-meter, an open-source cost meter that attaches to a live vLLM server and reports real \$/M-tokens against the operator's own traffic. We further show that FP8 quantization benefits the MoE architectures we tested roughly 2.2-2.4x more than the dense model (+69 to +74% vs. +31% peak throughput; n=3, broader validation needed), and our data are consistent with active parameter count, not total model size, being a primary predictor of saturation economics. To rule out single-hardware confounding we repeat the core sweep on A100 80GB PCIe (56 runs): the load-driven spread reproduces at 7.0-11.4x, the active-parameters ordering survives at FP8, and the dense-FP8 advantage inverts on silicon without native FP8 tensor cores -- a hardware-conditional caveat the framework already accommodates.

</details>


### [3] [Consensus Time in 3-Majority and 2-Choices Is Determined by the Maximum Initial Opinion Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11778)
*Niccolò D Archivio*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文确定了完全图上3-Majority和2-Choices动力学在同步模型下收敛时间的正确参数，证明了收敛时间主要由初始最大意见密度决定，而非意见总数k或初始分布的L2范数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作[Shimizu and Shiraga, PODC'25]提供了收敛时间上下界的弱匹配（仅在特定初始配置下成立），本文旨在获得强意义上的紧界，即对所有初始配置均成立的对数因子内的上下界匹配。

Method: 引入初始意见频率向量的无穷范数‖α(0)‖∞（即初始最大意见密度）作为关键参数，对两种动力学分别进行紧的上下界分析。

Result: 证明了3-Majority在高概率下Θ(min{‖α(0)‖∞⁻¹, √n})轮达成共识，2-Choices在高概率下Θ(‖α(0)‖∞⁻¹)轮达成共识，上下界在对数因子内匹配且对所有初始配置成立。

Conclusion: 两种动力学的收敛时间由"局部"参数‖α(0)‖∞（初始最大意见密度）决定，而非全局参数如意见数k或初始分布的平方L2范数。

Abstract: We establish the correct parameter governing the convergence time of the 3-Majority and 2-Choices dynamics on the complete graph in the synchronous model. Recent work [Shimizu and Shiraga, PODC'25] provides matching upper and lower bounds on the number of rounds to consensus, but only in a weak sense: the bounds are shown to coincide for some initial opinion configuration.  In contrast, we obtain tight bounds in a strong sense, with upper and lower bounds matching up to logarithmic factors for every initial configuration. Let $α$ (0) be the initial opinion-frequency vector, and denote by ___$α$ (0) ___ $\infty$ its maximum entry. We show that 3-Majority reaches consensus in $Θ$(min{___$α$ (0) ___ -1 $\infty$ , $\sqrt$ n}) rounds w.h.p., while 2-Choices reaches consensus in $Θ$(___$α$ (0) ___ -1 $\infty$ ) rounds w.h.p. Our results demonstrate that the convergence time of both dynamics is governed not by global parameters such as the number of opinions k or the squared ${\ell}$ 2 norm of the initial opinion distribution, but rather by the ''local'' parameter ___$α$ (0) ___ $\infty$ , the maximum initial opinion density.

</details>


### [4] [Optimizing Cloud Deployment: Blending of IaaS and FaaS for Microservice Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11824)
*Nikhil Kapoor,Sougata Mukherjea*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于指标的自动化框架，用于分析微服务性能并识别适合无服务器执行的微服务，从而实现从传统IaaS到混合IaaS+FaaS部署模式的迁移优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云计算的快速发展促使混合部署模式（结合IaaS和FaaS）的采用，以优化资源利用率、可扩展性和运营效率。然而，如何识别哪些微服务适合迁移到无服务器架构仍是一个挑战，因此需要一种系统化的方法来指导迁移决策。

Method: 开发了一个自动化框架，通过分析服务级性能指标来评估微服务特性；使用两个微服务应用作为案例研究，基于指标驱动的方法识别最适合无服务器执行的微服务，并实现从IaaS到混合IaaS+FaaS模式的迁移。

Result: 研究识别出了适合无服务器执行的微服务，成功实现了混合部署模式。研究结果揭示了不同云服务模型的优势和局限性，验证了所提出的自动化方法在优化云原生应用部署方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为云原生应用的优化部署提供了一种可扩展且可复制的自动化方法论，帮助组织做出更明智的云服务模型选择决策，最大化混合云部署的价值。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of cloud computing has resulted in the adoption of hybrid deployments that blend Infrastructure-as-a-Service (IaaS) and Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) service models to optimize resource utilization, scalability, and operational efficiency. This paper presents a comprehensive study and practical implementation of a metrics-driven approach for migrating microservices from a traditional IaaS service model to a hybrid IaaS + FaaS model, using two microservice applications as case studies. The research develops an automated framework to analyze service-level performance metrics to identify microservices that are best suited for serverless execution. The findings of our research highlight the benefits and limitations of different cloud service models and provide a scalable and replicable automated methodology for optimized deployment of cloud-native applications.

</details>


### [5] [Harnessing Routing Foresight for Micro-step-level MoE load balancing in RL Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11867)
*Yuming Zhou,Haoyang Li,Sheng Lin,Yanfeng Zhao,Tong Zhao,Xupeng Miao,Jie Jiang,Fangcheng Fu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ForeMoE针对MoE强化学习后训练中的专家负载不平衡问题，通过利用多阶段RL流水线中的可预见路由信息，实现了微步骤级别的负载均衡，在64 GPU上相比现有系统获得最高1.45倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE负载均衡系统针对预训练设计，依赖历史步骤级统计信息，但在强化学习后训练场景下失效：虽然步骤级负载稳定，但微步骤处理的小批量大小会导致严重的高频负载波动。

Method: 提出ForeMoE系统：1) 利用多阶段RL流水线（rollout、recompute、policy update）中rollout阶段可预见的路由信息，主动指导后续阶段的负载均衡；2) 采用分层规划器将NP难负载均衡问题分解为可处理的子问题；3) 设计利用CPU辅助和GPU直接传输等互补硬件路径的传输引擎，实现重叠专家传输。

Result: 在64个GPU上的评估表明，ForeMoE相比最先进的RL后训练系统实现了最高1.45倍的加速。

Conclusion: ForeMoE成功解决了MoE强化学习后训练中的微步骤级负载平衡问题，通过利用流水线中的可预见信息和分层优化策略，显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) and reinforcement learning (RL) post-training now dominate large language model (LLM) development, yet expert load imbalance remains a critical challenge. Existing load-balancing systems target pre-training by relying on historical step-level statistics. However, these methods fail under the unique workload dynamics of RL post-training: the step-level load is stable, but the tiny batch sizes processed during micro-steps cause severe, high-frequency load fluctuations.
  We introduce ForeMoE, a micro-step-level load balancing system for MoE RL post-training. Instead of relying on historical statistics, ForeMoE exploits the multi-stage RL pipeline (rollout, recompute, policy update) by using foreseeable routing information from the rollout stage to proactively guide load balancing in the remaining stages. To support frequent per-micro-step reconfiguration, ForeMoE employs a hierarchical planner that decomposes the NP-hard load balancing problem into tractable sub-components, alongside a transfer engine that leverages complementary hardware paths (CPU-assisted and GPU-direct) for overlapped expert transfer. Evaluations on 64 GPUs demonstrate that ForeMoE achieves up to a 1.45$\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art RL post-training systems.

</details>


### [6] [From Fork-Join to Asynchronous Tasks: Parallelizing Tiled Cholesky Decomposition with OpenMP and HPX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11937)
*Alexander Strack,Alexander Van Craen,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过Cholesky-Bench基准测试量化比较了异步多任务运行时(HPX)与OpenMP在非规则并行负载上的性能差异，发现HPX相比OpenMP最优配置可获得15%-30%的性能提升，异步任务开销降低约3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fork-join并行模型(如OpenMP)的隐式同步屏障会惩罚非均匀工作负载的算法，而异步多任务(AMT)运行时通过细粒度任务依赖图规避这些屏障。然而，AMT相对于精心编写的fork-join基线的实际性能优势很少被量化评估。

Method: 引入Cholesky-Bench基准测试工具，以分块Cholesky分解这一典型非规则内核为研究对象，比较右视算法的四种并行化变体：经典fork-join、压缩fork-join(暴露内层循环并行性)、同步任务、异步任务(显式数据依赖)。在双路128核AMD Zen 2节点上测试GCC/LLVM的OpenMP实现与HPX AMT运行时，涵盖多种分块大小和问题规模。

Result: 在最优分块大小下，HPX在所有变体中比OpenMP快15%-30%；异步HPX任务比OpenMP对应版本快达26%，任务开销降低约3.8倍；压缩fork-join变体性能接近同步任务；移除冗余同步屏障可带来7%(OpenMP)到14%(HPX)的额外提升；GCC与LLVM在fork-join调度和任务创建开销上存在编译器特定差异。

Conclusion: AMT运行时相比传统fork-join模型具有显著性能优势，主要源于更低的任务开销和消除隐式同步屏障；通过优化并行化策略(如压缩fork-join、移除冗余屏障)可大幅缩小两种模型的性能差距；编译器实现细节对fork-join性能有重要影响。

Abstract: Fork-join parallelism, popularized by OpenMP, remains the dominant model for shared-memory parallel programming, but its implicit synchronization barriers can penalize algorithms with inhomogeneous workloads. Asynchronous many-task (AMT) runtimes sidestep these barriers by expressing work as a dependency graph of fine-grained tasks. Yet, the actual performance benefit over a carefully written fork-join baseline is rarely quantified. In this work, we introduce Cholesky-Bench and use it to revisit the tiled Cholesky decomposition, a canonical irregular kernel, comparing four parallelization variants of the right-looking algorithm across two runtimes: the OpenMP implementations shipped with GCC and LLVM, and the HPX AMT runtime. The variants span classical fork-join, a collapsed fork-join that exposes additional inner-loop parallelism, synchronous tasking, and asynchronous tasking with explicit data dependencies. We benchmark all eight combinations on a dual-socket 128-core AMD Zen 2 node across multiple tile sizes and problem sizes. Our results show that across all variants, HPX outperforms OpenMP at the optimal tile size by 15%-30%. Specifically, asynchronous HPX tasks are up to 26% faster than their OpenMP counterparts, and exhibit roughly 3.8x smaller task overhead. Furthermore, the collapsed fork-join variants close most of the gap to synchronous tasking. Removing redundant synchronization barriers yields an additional improvement of 7% (OpenMP) to 14% (HPX). A GCC-versus-LLVM comparison further reveals compiler-specific differences in fork-join scheduling and task-creation overheads.

</details>


### [7] [The PM-EdgeMap: Towards Real-Time Process Mining on the Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12103)
*Hendrik Reiter,Christian Imenkamp,Olaf Landsiedel,Andrea Maldonado,Patrick Rathje,Wilhelm Hasselbring*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在边缘计算环境下进行实时流程挖掘的可行性，通过案例研究验证了该方法能够有效支持智能工厂的自主控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能工厂正向信息物理系统(CPS)演进，对自主性需求日益增加，这要求基于传感器数据进行实时决策。流程挖掘能够提供有价值的洞察，边缘计算范式通过利用附近的计算资源支持实时性要求，因此需要研究在边缘端执行实时流程挖掘算法的影响。

Method: 首先提出了描述相关数据集和计算拓扑的形式化方法，然后通过一个基于边缘的合规性检查算法案例研究来评估边缘计算方法。

Result: 案例研究结果证明了基于边缘的实时流程挖掘的可行性，展示了其在增强智能工厂自主控制方面的优势。

Conclusion: 基于边缘的实时流程挖掘对于增强智能工厂的自主控制是可行且有益的，能够有效满足智能工厂向信息物理系统演进过程中的实时决策需求。

Abstract: Smart factories are evolving into Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS), demanding increased autonomy. This necessitates real-time decision making, facilitated by insights derived from sensor data. Process mining offers a valuable approach to gain such insights and guide actions. The edge computing paradigm supports this real-time requirement by enabling network communication between sensors and leveraging nearby computing resources. This paper investigates the implications of performing real-time process mining algorithms on the edge. Within this paper, we first propose a formalism to describe relevant datasets and the computing topology. We then evaluate the edge computing approach through a case study involving an edge-based conformance checking algorithm. The results demonstrate the feasibility and benefits of edge-based real-time process mining for enhanced autonomous control in smart factories.

</details>


### [8] [Efficient and Robust Online Learning to Rank in Decentralized Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12246)
*Marcel Gregoriadis,Martijn de Vos,Sayan Biswas,Anne-Marie Kermarrec,Johan Pouwelse*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了RankGuard，首个去中心化在线学习排序框架，通过利用用户私有点击历史防御投毒攻击，并提供了理论收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线学习排序系统依赖中心化服务器，存在运营商引入违背用户利益偏置的风险。去中心化学习虽可解决此问题，但面临恶意节点发送投毒模型更新降低排序质量的安全威胁。

Method: 提出RankGuard框架，用户协作训练排序模型并直接交换模型更新。防御机制：利用用户私有点击历史（经位置偏置校正）评估传入模型，仅当传入模型比当前本地模型能更好地解释用户过去交互时才进行聚合。这种机制使恶意节点难以在通过测试的同时不真正帮助用户。

Result: 在四个标准基准数据集、三种点击模型和四种投毒攻击（包括强自适应攻击）上的实验表明，RankGuard在大多数情况下优于所有基线，且比最接近的竞争对手效率提高高达62倍。

Conclusion: RankGuard成功实现了去中心化在线学习排序，有效防御投毒攻击，首次为去中心化OLTR算法提供了形式化收敛性分析，在安全性和效率方面均表现优异。

Abstract: In Online Learning to Rank (OLTR), ranking models are trained directly from live user interactions, but existing systems rely on a trusted central server to collect and process these interactions. This leaves operators free to introduce biases that conflict with user interests. Decentralized learning offers an attractive alternative, allowing users to collaboratively train a shared ranking model by exchanging model updates directly with one another, without any central authority. In such settings, however, malicious nodes can send poisoned model updates that degrade the ranking quality of honest nodes. We introduce RankGuard, a decentralized OLTR framework in which users collaboratively train ranking models and exchange model updates directly with other nodes. RankGuard defends against poisoning attacks by carefully evaluating incoming models against the user's own private click history, corrected for position bias. An incoming model is only aggregated if it better explains the user's past interactions than the current local model, making it fundamentally hard for malicious nodes to craft updates that pass this test without also genuinely helping the user. We derive a theoretical convergence guarantee of RankGuard. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first formal convergence analysis of a decentralized OLTR algorithm. We evaluate RankGuard against four poisoning attacks, including a powerful adaptive attack, using four standard benchmarks and three click models. RankGuard outperforms all baselines in most settings while being up to 62x more efficient than its closest competitors.

</details>


### [9] [Fair Comparison of Scheduling Algorithms on Heterogeneous Edge Clusters: A Continuous Adaptive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12343)
*Zihang Wang,Boris Sedlak,Juan Luis Herrera,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源基准测试平台，用于公平比较边缘-云连续体中的连续多模式调度（CMMS）算法，发现控制器排名高度依赖配置：轻负载下表现最佳的深度强化学习算法在重负载下输给基于规则的启发式算法近29个百分点，且决策开销高出约500倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMMS算法研究难以公平比较，因为每个研究通常在自己的技术栈中评估控制器、仅在单一工作负载下测试、且不报告每决策开销。这导致缺乏统一的比较框架来评估调度算法在满足多维SLO（延迟、吞吐量、输出质量）方面的真实性能。

Method: 提出开源基准测试平台，包含：(i) 统一控制器接口；(ii) 支持多种工作负载模式的闭环工作负载驱动器；(iii) 双指标SLO评分，分别报告原始SLO（整体合规性）和稳态SLO（稳定运行期间合规性）。在五种集群配置和两种负载机制下运行六种控制器，共进行424轮实验。

Result: 控制器排名强烈依赖配置：在轻负载下获胜的深度强化学习控制器在负载增加时输给基于规则的启发式控制器近29个百分点，且每决策操作开销约高出500倍。此外，将原始SLO与稳态SLO分离可以揭示单一聚合分数会混淆的切换成本。

Conclusion: CMMS算法的评估需要综合考虑配置依赖性、决策开销和多维度SLO指标。平台的双指标SLO评分方法能够更准确地揭示算法在实际部署中的真实性能，避免单一指标掩盖重要的性能权衡。

Abstract: Modern Artificial Intelligence (AI) workloads deployed across the heterogeneous tiers of an edge--cloud continuum must satisfy multi-dimensional Service Level Objectives (SLOs) over latency, throughput, and output quality. For each incoming task, the scheduler picks both a target node and a processing mode (e.g., full or reduced inference precision). We call this class of problems \emph{Continuous Multi-Mode Scheduling} (CMMS). Comparing CMMS algorithms fairly is difficult because prior studies typically evaluate each controller in its own stack, under a single workload, and without reporting per-decision overhead. To close these gaps, we present an open source benchmark platform that features (i) a unified controller interface, (ii) a closed-loop workload driver covering multiple workload patterns, and (iii) dual-metric SLO scoring that reports raw SLO (overall compliance) and steady-state SLO (compliance during stable operation) separately. Running six controllers across five cluster configurations and two load regimes (424 episodes), we find that controller rankings are strongly configuration-dependent: a deep reinforcement-learning winner under light workloads loses to a rule-based heuristic by nearly 29 percentage points once load intensifies, at roughly 500$\times$ the per-decision operational overhead. We further show that separating raw from steady-state SLOs exposes switching costs that a single aggregate score would otherwise conflate.

</details>

### [10] [SPEAR: A System for Post-Quantization Error-Adaptive Recovery Enabling Efficient Low-Bit LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11244)
*Hongyuan Liu,Yawei Li,Zhiqiang Que,Qinli Yang,Junming Shao,Guosheng Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SPEAR系统，通过自适应的错误补偿机制，在几乎不增加内存开销的情况下，将W4量化与FP16之间的困惑度差距缩小了56-75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM量化方法即使在4-bit下也存在明显的质量差距，特别是在小模型中更为显著。其根本原因在于量化误差具有高度的输入依赖性且在不同token间变化显著，而现有的后量化补偿方法是静态的，对所有输入应用相同的校正，导致简单token被过度校正而困难token校正不足。

Method: 提出SPEAR系统，引入轻量级的错误补偿器（ECs），通过per-token门控机制进行调制，并仅放置在通过CKA引导的熵感知诊断识别出的最误差敏感层。同时采用自适应核融合调度（包括集成后处理的peer-reduction核和P2P双写）以及SLO约束的EC感知调度器来解决部署中的计算开销、张量并行同步和延迟不稳定性问题。

Result: 在具有挑战性的per-channel量化设置下，SPEAR恢复了W4与FP16之间56-75%的困惑度差距，同时增加不到1%的模型内存开销，并保持与广泛使用的4-bit服务部署相当的延迟。

Conclusion: SPEAR成功实现了低比特LLM服务的质量-成本权衡优化，证明了输入自适应的错误补偿机制能有效弥补量化带来的质量损失，为高效的LLM部署提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient large language model (LLM) serving is increasingly constrained by deployment cost. Quantization is a key technique for reducing serving cost, yet even state-of-the-art 4-bit quantizers exhibit a noticeable quality gap from FP16, particularly for smaller models where low-bit serving is most beneficial. We identify a fundamental cause of this gap: quantization error is highly input-dependent and varies substantially across tokens, while existing post-quantization compensation methods are static and apply identical corrections to all inputs. As a result, easy tokens are over-corrected while hard tokens remain under-corrected.
  We present SPEAR, a system for post-quantization error-adaptive recovery that improves low-bit LLM serving. SPEAR introduces lightweight Error Compensators (ECs) modulated by per-token gates and places them only at the most error-sensitive layers identified through a CKA-guided entropy-aware diagnostic. This focuses a small parameter budget where it is most effective.
  Efficient deployment of ECs presents several systems challenges, including additional computation, tensor-parallel synchronization caused by input-dependent gating, and latency instability across configurations. SPEAR addresses these issues through adaptive kernel-fusion dispatch, combining an epilogue-integrated peer-reduction kernel with P2P dual-write to fuse the post-EC computation into low-bit GEMMs, and an SLO-constrained EC-aware scheduler for predictable serving performance.
  Across challenging per-channel quantization settings, SPEAR recovers 56-75% of the perplexity gap between W4 and FP16 while adding less than 1% model memory overhead and maintaining latency comparable to a widely used 4-bit serving deployment.

</details>


### [11] [A Fast Locality Simulator for GEMM Design-Space Exploration on Multi-Chiplet GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11716)
*Euijun Chung,Hyesoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多芯片GPU的快速局部性模拟器，用于优化LLM中的GEMM操作。研究发现CTA遍历顺序是影响芯片间流量的关键设计因素，使用2D块交换遍历相比最佳1D遍历可减少高达5.1倍的远程流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多芯片GPU将内存分割为本地和远程HBM区域，减少远程HBM流量对性能和能效至关重要。对于LLM中主导的GEMM算子，内核选择（如操作数布局、CTA遍历顺序、数据放置）会显著影响芯片间流量，而最小化远程访问的最优策略并不直观。

Method: 开发了一个快速的、功能性的瓦片级局部性模拟器，对CTA调度、每芯片L2缓存以及本地/远程HBM访问进行建模，用于评估全尺寸LLM GEMM配置。基于模拟器反馈，利用智能体AI探索不同的优化策略。

Result: 在代表性LLM GEMM测试中，相同GEMM维度下设计空间中的远程流量差异高达90倍。使用2D块交换CTA遍历在轮询放置策略下，相比最佳1D遍历可减少高达5.1倍的远程流量。

Conclusion: CTA遍历顺序是影响芯片间流量的一阶、GEMM相关设计因素，通过模拟器指导的优化策略可以显著降低多芯片GPU中的远程内存访问开销。

Abstract: Multi-chiplet GPUs split memory into local and remote HBM regions across a silicon interposer, and reducing the remote HBM traffic is crucial for the performance and energy efficiency of multi-chiplet GPUs. For general matrix multiplication (GEMM), the dominant operator in large language models (LLMs), the resulting inter-chiplet traffic depends strongly on kernel choices such as operand layout, CTA traversal order, and data placement, and the optimal strategy to minimize remote accesses is nontrivial. We present a fast, functional, tile-level locality simulator that models CTA scheduling, per-chiplet L2 caches, and local/remote HBM accesses to evaluate a full-size LLM GEMM configuration. Across representative LLM GEMMs, the simulator shows that remote traffic varies by up to 90x across the design space for the same GEMM dimensions. Moreover, using the simulator as feedback, an agentic AI discovers that a 2D block-swizzle CTA traversal reduces remote traffic over the best 1D traversal by up to 5.1x under round-robin placement, identifying CTA traversal order as a first-order, GEMM-dependent design knob for inter-chiplet traffic.

</details>


### [12] [Making Locality-aware GEMM Compatible with Page-Granularity Placement on Chiplet GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11718)
*Euijun Chung,Jae Hyung Ju,Hyesoon Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出Chiplet-Contiguous Layout，一种针对多芯片GPU的全局内存布局，有效解决了GEMM中局部性感知数据放置与固定页粒度交错不兼容的问题，在LLM推理和训练中显著减少远程HBM流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多芯片GPU通过扩展计算吞吐量和HBM容量提升性能，但其非统一内存系统使得芯片间的局部性对性能和能效至关重要。然而，在通用矩阵乘法（GEMM）中，局部性感知数据放置往往与固定页粒度数据交错不兼容，因为不同工作负载间映射数据的最佳粒度差异很大。

Method: 提出Chiplet-Contiguous Layout，一种全局内存布局，将芯片本地数据连续存储。该布局无需更改操作系统或硬件，即可实现与页粒度放置兼容的局部性感知放置，适用于各种大型语言模型（LLM）GEMM形状。

Result: 在Qwen 3 30B和Llama 3.1 70B的代表性LLM推理和训练GEMM上，Chiplet-Contiguous Layout相比4KB交错平均减少远程HBM流量24.7倍（Qwen）和19.2倍（Llama），相比粗粒度局部性感知放置分别减少4.1倍和2.1倍。

Conclusion: Chiplet-Contiguous Layout成功解决了多芯片GPU中GEMM的数据局部性优化问题，在不修改系统软件和硬件的前提下，显著提升了内存访问效率，为LLM推理和训练性能优化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multi-chiplet GPUs scale compute throughput and high-bandwidth memory (HBM) capacity, but their non-uniform memory system makes locality between chiplets and their data critical to the GPU's performance and energy efficiency. Locality-aware scheduling and data placement identify which data should reside near each chiplet. However, in general matrix multiplication (GEMM), locality-aware data placement often becomes incompatible with a fixed page-granularity data interleaving, since the optimal granularity for mapping data across chiplets varies widely across workloads. We propose Chiplet-Contiguous Layout, a global memory layout that stores chiplet-local data contiguously. Chiplet-Contiguous Layout enables locality-aware placement compatible with page-granularity placement across diverse large language model (LLM) GEMM shapes, without changes to the operating system or hardware. On representative LLM inference and training GEMMs from Qwen 3 30B and Llama 3.1 70B, Chiplet-Contiguous Layout on average reduces remote HBM traffic by 24.7x on Qwen and 19.2x on Llama over 4KB interleaving, and by 4.1x and 2.1x over coarse locality-aware placement.

</details>


### [13] [BenDi: An Energy-Efficient Quasi-Stochastic Systolic Architecture for Edge Bioelectronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12235)
*Bochen Ye,Yihan Pan,Shady Agwa,Themis Prodromakis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出BenDi，一种面向边缘生物电子系统的准随机脉动阵列架构，通过多级优化（低电压、Bent-Pyramid数据格式、DiP数据流、硬件感知量化）实现了3.35倍面积缩减和5倍能效提升，代价是1%-3.3%的精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续长期监测ECG等生物医学信号有助于缓解公共健康威胁，但CNN等AI模型计算资源需求高，难以部署在资源受限的边缘设备上，因此需要一种能效高、面积小且保持高精度的硬件架构。

Method: 提出BenDi架构，采用多级优化策略：电路层低电压供电；Bent-Pyramid数据格式实现准随机乘法；DiP脉动数据流优化；硬件感知量化处理CNN，以在有限硬件预算内实现高精度推理。

Result: 在22nm工艺、0.5V/100MHz条件下，相比最先进二值权重固定脉动阵列，BenDi面积减小3.35倍，能效提升5倍；相比同类生物电子边缘系统，能效和面积效率均提升一个数量级，精度损失为1%-3.3%（MIT-BIH和Apnea-ECG基准）。

Conclusion: BenDi成功在边缘资源受限环境中实现了高效CNN推理，以较小的精度损失换取了显著的面积和能效提升，为生物医学信号的边缘持续监测提供了可行的硬件解决方案。

Abstract: Continuous long-term monitoring and diagnosis of biomedical signals, such as electrocardiograms (ECGs), can help mitigate an increasing threat to public health. Artificial Intelligence (AI) models, such as Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), provide accurate monitoring and classification for relevant diseases; however, they require more computational resources than conventional AI hardware can typically afford, especially for a resource-constrained environment on the edge. In this work, we present BenDi, an energy-efficient quasi-stochastic systolic architecture for bioelectronic systems on the edge. BenDi leverages multiple levels of energy and power optimization, ranging from circuits to software quantization, including low supply voltage, the \underline{Ben}t-Pyramid data format for quasi-stochastic multiplication, the \underline{Di}P systolic dataflow, and hardware-aware quantization, to handle CNNs with high accuracy on the edge within limited hardware budgets. The hardware implementation results, using a commercial 22nm technology, show that BenDi architecture, at 0.5 Voltage and 100 MHz, offers 3.35x smaller area and 5x higher energy efficiency, compared to state-of-the-art binary-based weight-stationary systolic architectures. Regarding Bioelectronic edge systems, BenDi achieves an order-of-magnitude improvement in energy efficiency and another order-of-magnitude improvement in area efficiency, compared to its counterparts. This significant improvement comes at the cost of 1\% to 3.3\% accuracy loss on the MIT-BIH and Apnea-ECG benchmarks, respectively, compared with conventional computing using the 32-bit floating-point format.

</details>
